    <p class="text-muted-foreground mb-3 leading-relaxed">Twenty years ago, Windows 95 was the most common operating system. Yahoo and Altavista were our gateways to the internet. Steve Jobs just returned to Apple. Google didn&#x27;t exist yet. America Online had just launched their Instant Messenger. IPv6 was coming soon. That&#x27;s part of the state of softwar...</p>

Now, when I read that headline my first thought was that it was 100 people over 6 months and a statistical fluke. But no, they followed a quarter million Britons for 5 years.</p>

    <p class="text-muted-foreground mb-3 leading-relaxed">In the aftermath of Wannacry, there&#x27;s a lot of discussion of organizations not updating their systems. There are two main reasons organizations don&#x27;t update the operating systems they run: compatibility and training. Training is simpler — you have to train people about the changes to the Start Me...</p>
    <a href='https://shostack.org/blog/maintaining-updating-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' class="read-more-btn">Read Full Article →</a>

<details class="group border-b border-border/50 py-1">
  <summary class="cursor-pointer hover:bg-secondary/30 transition-colors list-none flex items-center justify-between py-1.5 px-2">
    <div class="flex-1 overflow-hidden mr-4">
      <span class="text-sm font-medium text-foreground">Certificate pinning is great in stone soup </span>
    </div>
    <span class="text-muted-foreground text-xs shrink-0 group-open:rotate-180 transition-transform">▼</span>
  </summary>
  <div class="p-3 bg-secondary/10 rounded-md mt-1 mb-2 text-sm mx-2">
    <p class="text-muted-foreground mb-3 leading-relaxed">In his &quot; ground rules &quot; article, Mordaxus gives us the phrase &quot;stone soup security,&quot; where everyone brings a little bit and throws it into the pot. I always try to follow Warren Buffet&#x27;s advice, to praise specifically and criticize in general.</p>
